Motivation: 恶意软件特别是使用混淆技术的恶意软件仍然是组织面临的主要操作风险。尽管机器学习检测方法不断发展，但公共数据集缺乏特征兼容性，限制了模型在面对分布偏移时的泛化能力以及在不同数据集间的可迁移性。

Method: 研究采用统一的数据预处理管道处理EMBERv2（2,381维）特征数据集，在两种训练设置下训练配对模型：EMBER + BODMAS 和 EMBER + BODMAS + ERMDS。模型评估时，两种模型均在TRITIUM、INFERNO和SOREL-20M数据集上进行测试，其中EMBER + BODMAS设置还额外使用ERMDS进行测试。

Result: 摘要中未提供具体的实验结果数据，但描述了完整的实验设计和评估框架，表明研究系统性地评估了不同预处理方法在多个恶意软件数据集上的检测性能。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统性的实验设计，旨在解决恶意软件检测中数据集特征兼容性不足的问题，评估不同数据预处理方法对模型泛化能力和可迁移性的影响，为改进PE文件恶意软件检测提供方法学参考。

Abstract: Malware continues to be a predominant operational risk for organizations, especially when obfuscation techniques are used to evade detection. Despite the ongoing efforts in the development of Machine Learning (ML) detection approaches, there is still a lack of feature compatibility in public datasets. This limits generalization when facing distribution shifts, as well as transferability to different datasets. This study evaluates the suitability of different data preprocessing approaches for the detection of Portable Executable (PE) files with ML models. The preprocessing pipeline unifies EMBERv2 (2,381-dim) features datasets, trains paired models under two training setups: EMBER + BODMAS and EMBER + BODMAS + ERMDS. Regarding model evaluation, both EMBER + BODMAS and EMBER + BODMAS + ERMDS models are tested against TRITIUM, INFERNO and SOREL-20M. ERMDS is also used for testing for the EMBER + BODMAS setup.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Beyond identifiability: Learning causal representations with few environments and finite samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25796)
*Inbeom Lee,Tongtong Jin,Bryon Aragam*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为因果表示学习提供了有限样本保证，证明仅需次线性数量的环境（对数级未知干预）即可学习因果表示，无需预先设计干预目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习旨在为通用表示学习问题提供严谨基础，通过将因果模型与潜在因子模型结合来学习具有因果语义的可解释表示。尽管因果表示学习的可识别性理论已有发展，但估计和有限样本边界方面的理解仍不足。

Method: 通过细致的扰动分析，提出新分析方法，证明仅需对数级数量的未知、多节点干预即可学习因果表示，且干预目标无需预先精心设计。

Result: 该方法能够保证一致恢复：(a) 潜在因果图，(b) 混合矩阵和表示，以及(c) 未知的干预目标。

Conclusion: 该研究为因果表示学习提供了明确的有限样本保证，显著降低了所需环境数量（从线性降至次线性），并放宽了对干预目标设计的严格要求，推进了该领域的理论发展。

Abstract: We provide explicit, finite-sample guarantees for learning causal representations from data with a sublinear number of environments. Causal representation learning seeks to provide a rigourous foundation for the general representation learning problem by bridging causal models with latent factor models in order to learn interpretable representations with causal semantics. Despite a blossoming theory of identifiability in causal representation learning, estimation and finite-sample bounds are less well understood. We show that causal representations can be learned with only a logarithmic number of unknown, multi-node interventions, and that the intervention targets need not be carefully designed in advance. Through a careful perturbation analysis, we provide a new analysis of this problem that guarantees consistent recovery of (a) the latent causal graph, (b) the mixing matrix and representations, and (c) \emph{unknown} intervention targets.

</details>


### [113] [On the Expressive Power of Contextual Relations in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25860)
*Demián Fraiman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于测度论的上下文表示框架，将文本建模为语义嵌入空间上的概率测度，词间上下文关系表示为耦合测度，并引入Sinkhorn Transformer架构证明其通用逼近能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构在自然语言建模中表现出色，但其表达能力的精确数学刻画仍不完整。需要建立理论框架来形式化描述上下文表示，特别是词与词之间的语义关系。

Method: 引入测度论框架：将文本建模为语义嵌入空间上的概率测度，词间上下文关系表示为耦合测度。提出Sinkhorn Transformer架构，这是一种类似Transformer的结构。

Result: 证明了通用逼近定理：任何编码语义关系耦合测度的连续耦合函数，都可以用具有适当参数的Sinkhorn Transformer进行一致逼近。

Conclusion: 该工作为Transformer的上下文建模能力提供了严格的数学基础，通过测度论框架形式化了语义关系表示，并证明了所提架构的理论表达能力。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have achieved remarkable empirical success in modeling contextual relationships in natural language, yet a precise mathematical characterization of their expressive power remains incomplete. In this work, we introduce a measure-theoretic framework for contextual representations in which texts are modeled as probability measures over a semantic embedding space, and contextual relations between words, are represented as coupling measures between them. Within this setting, we introduce Sinkhorn Transformer, a transformer-like architecture. Our main result is a universal approximation theorem: any continuous coupling function between probability measures, that encodes the semantic relation coupling measure, can be uniformly approximated by a Sinkhorn Transformer with appropriate parameters.

</details>


### [114] [Privacy-Accuracy Trade-offs in High-Dimensional LASSO under Perturbation Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26227)
*Ayaka Sakata,Haruka Tanzawa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文使用近似消息传递(AMP)分析高维稀疏LASSO回归在差分隐私机制下的隐私-准确性权衡，发现稀疏性在隐私保护中起关键作用，且输出扰动和目标扰动机制表现出不同的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维稀疏线性回归中的隐私保护问题，特别是LASSO估计器在差分隐私机制下的表现。需要理解稀疏性如何影响隐私-准确性权衡，以及不同隐私机制的行为差异。

Method: 使用近似消息传递(AMP)分析随机设计下的隐私噪声影响。研究两种差分隐私机制：输出扰动（向估计器注入噪声）和目标扰动（向损失函数添加随机线性项）。采用典型情况度量，包括平均KL散度，从假设检验角度量化隐私。

Result: 稀疏性在隐私-准确性权衡中起核心作用：更强的正则化可以通过稳定估计器来改善隐私。两种机制表现出质的不同行为：目标扰动中增加噪声水平可能产生非单调效应，过度噪声可能使估计器失稳，增加对数据扰动的敏感性。

Conclusion: AMP为分析高维稀疏模型中的隐私-准确性权衡提供了强大框架。稀疏性显著影响隐私保护效果，不同隐私机制需要不同的优化策略，特别是在目标扰动中需要谨慎控制噪声水平以避免反效果。

Abstract: We study privacy-preserving sparse linear regression in the high-dimensional regime, focusing on the LASSO estimator. We analyze two widely used mechanisms for differential privacy: output perturbation, which injects noise into the estimator, and objective perturbation, which adds a random linear term to the loss function. Using approximate message passing (AMP), we characterize the typical behavior of these estimators under random design and privacy noise. To quantify privacy, we adopt typical-case measures, including the on-average KL divergence, which admits a hypothesis-testing interpretation in terms of distinguishability between neighboring datasets. Our analysis reveals that sparsity plays a central role in shaping the privacy-accuracy trade-off: stronger regularization can improve privacy by stabilizing the estimator against single-point data changes. We further show that the two mechanisms exhibit qualitatively different behaviors. In particular, for objective perturbation, increasing the noise level can have non-monotonic effects, and excessive noise may destabilize the estimator, leading to increased sensitivity to data perturbations. Our results demonstrate that AMP provides a powerful framework for analyzing privacy-accuracy trade-offs in high-dimensional sparse models.

</details>


### [115] [A Power-Weighted Noncentral Complex Gaussian Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26344)
*Toru Nakashika*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种新的复值随机变量概率模型——幂加权非中心复高斯分布，该模型直接在复平面上构建，通过单一形状参数控制分布几何结构，统一了Rice、Nakagami和gamma等常用分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复高斯分布难以表示单个源信号的多样化幅度特性，而现有的非高斯幅度分布虽然经验拟合效果好，但未能明确考虑复值观测固有的复平面几何结构。

Method: 提出幂加权非中心复高斯分布模型，直接在复平面上构建，通过单一形状参数引入非线性相位扩散，控制分布几何从沿相位方向的弧状扩散到向原点集中的连续变化。

Result: 推导的幅度和功率分布为信号建模提供了统一框架，涵盖了Rice、Nakagami和gamma等广泛使用的分布。在语音功率谱实验中，该模型在似然度方面始终优于传统分布。

Conclusion: 提出的新模型在保持复值观测几何结构的同时，通过单一参数灵活控制分布形状，为复值信号建模提供了更强大且统一的概率框架。

Abstract: The complex Gaussian distribution has been widely used as a fundamental spectral and noise model in signal processing and communication. However, its Gaussian structure often limits its ability to represent the diverse amplitude characteristics observed in individual source signals. On the other hand, many existing non-Gaussian amplitude distributions derived from hyperspherical models achieve good empirical fit due to their power-law structures, while they do not explicitly account for the complex-plane geometry inherent in complex-valued observations. In this paper, we propose a new probabilistic model for complex-valued random variables, which can be interpreted as a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian distribution. Unlike conventional hyperspherical amplitude models, the proposed model is formulated directly on the complex plane and preserves the geometric structure of complex-valued observations while retaining a higher-dimensional interpretation. The model introduces a nonlinear phase diffusion through a single shape parameter, enabling continuous control of the distributional geometry from arc-shaped diffusion along the phase direction to concentration of probability mass toward the origin. We formulate the proposed distribution and analyze the statistical properties of the induced amplitude distribution. The derived amplitude and power distributions provide a unified framework encompassing several widely used distributions in signal modeling, including the Rice, Nakagami, and gamma distributions. Experimental results on speech power spectra demonstrate that the proposed model consistently outperforms conventional distributions in terms of log-likelihood.

</details>


### [116] [Generative Score Inference for Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26349)
*Xinyu Tian,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Generative Score Inference (GSI)框架，利用深度生成模型生成合成样本来近似条件得分分布，为多模态学习提供统计有效且信息丰富的预测和置信集，在幻觉检测和图像字幕不确定性估计中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不确定性量化方法存在显著局限性，包括刚性假设和有限泛化能力，限制了其在多样化监督学习任务中的有效性。特别是在处理图像和文本等复杂多模态数据时，需要更灵活、通用的不确定性量化框架。

Method: 提出Generative Score Inference (GSI)框架，利用深度生成模型生成合成样本来近似条件得分分布，从而构建统计有效且信息丰富的预测和置信集。该方法不施加对数据或任务的限制性假设，能够适应广泛的多模态学习问题。

Result: 在两个代表性场景中验证GSI：大型语言模型幻觉检测和图像字幕不确定性估计。在幻觉检测方面达到最先进性能，在图像字幕中实现稳健的预测不确定性。性能受底层生成模型质量的正向影响。

Conclusion: GSI作为一种灵活推理框架，显著增强了多模态学习中的不确定性量化和可信度，具有作为通用不确定性量化工具的潜力，其性能与生成模型质量直接相关。

Abstract: Accurate uncertainty quantification is crucial for making reliable decisions in various supervised learning scenarios, particularly when dealing with complex, multimodal data such as images and text. Current approaches often face notable limitations, including rigid assumptions and limited generalizability, constraining their effectiveness across diverse supervised learning tasks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Generative Score Inference (GSI), a flexible inference framework capable of constructing statistically valid and informative prediction and confidence sets across a wide range of multimodal learning problems. GSI utilizes synthetic samples generated by deep generative models to approximate conditional score distributions, facilitating precise uncertainty quantification without imposing restrictive assumptions about the data or tasks. We empirically validate GSI's capabilities through two representative scenarios: hallucination detection in large language models and uncertainty estimation in image captioning. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in hallucination detection and robust predictive uncertainty in image captioning, and its performance is positively influenced by the quality of the underlying generative model. These findings underscore the potential of GSI as a versatile inference framework, significantly enhancing uncertainty quantification and trustworthiness in multimodal learning.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Finite-Time Observability of Oscillatory Instabilities in Synchronous p-bit Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25910)
*Naoya Onizawa,Shunsuke Koshita,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一个有限时间可观测性框架，用于分析同步tick-random p-bit退火中的振荡问题，从渐近稳定性转向实际可检测性，为选择更新概率提供图感知基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步更新方案在p-bit退火中提供了大规模并行化的自然途径，但可能引发周期-2振荡，从而降低优化性能。现有分析主要关注渐近稳定性，无法直接回答在有限时长退火过程中振荡模式何时实际出现这一实验相关问题。

Method: 开发了同步tick-random p-bit动力学的有限时间可观测性框架。从线性化平均场描述出发，推导出图依赖的判据，预测不稳定模式在有限观测窗口内是否放大到足以在一步自相关和能量轨迹等量中产生可见特征。

Result: 预测的阈值捕捉了振荡开始的图依赖性以及振荡变得实际可观测的有限时间条件。在G-set基准图和示例图族上验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架将分析从渐近不稳定性转向实际可检测性，为选择同步p-bit退火器的更新概率提供了图感知基础，无需依赖详尽的逐实例参数扫描。

Abstract: Synchronous update schemes in p-bit annealing offer a natural route to massive parallelism, but they can also induce period-2 oscillations that degrade optimization performance. In practical solvers, such oscillations matter only if they become observable within the finite runtime of the device or simulation, yet most existing analyses are formulated in terms of asymptotic stability. As a result, they do not directly address the experimentally relevant question of when oscillatory modes actually appear during finite-duration annealing. Here we develop a finite-time observability framework for synchronous tick-random p-bit dynamics. Starting from a linearized mean-field description, we derive a graph-dependent criterion that predicts whether unstable modes amplify enough within a finite observation window to produce visible signatures in quantities such as the one-step autocorrelation and the energy trace. This shifts the analysis from asymptotic instability to practical detectability and yields a principled estimate of the minimum reduction in synchrony required to suppress oscillations. We validate the framework on G-set benchmark graphs and illustrative graph families. The predicted thresholds capture both the graph dependence of oscillation onset and the finite-time conditions under which oscillations become practically observable. These results provide a graph-aware basis for selecting the update probability in synchronous p-bit annealers without relying on exhaustive instance-by-instance parameter sweeps.

</details>


### [118] [Sharp Concentration Inequalities: Phase Transition and Mixing of Orlicz Tails with Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25934)
*Yinan Shen,Jinchi Lv*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了亚韦布尔随机变量的尖锐集中不等式，揭示了在α=2处的相变现象，并提出了包含方差和Orlicz Ψα尾混合的新理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中不等式在处理亚韦布尔随机变量时存在局限性，特别是在标准差与Orlicz范数差异较大时，需要更精确的理论框架来区分这两种量度，以获得更尖锐的集中界限。

Method: 建立新的理论分析框架，推导出包含方差和Orlicz Ψα尾混合的集中不等式，通过min和max函数区分α≥2和1≤α≤2两种情况，并应用于鞅、随机向量、随机矩阵和协方差矩阵估计。

Result: 发现了α=2处的相变现象：对于α≥2，上界为2exp(-Cmax{t²/(n‖X‖²_Ψα), t^α/(n^{α-1}‖X‖^α_Ψα)})；对于1≤α≤2，上界为2exp(-Cmin{t²/(n‖X‖²_Ψα), t^α/(n^{α-1}‖X‖^α_Ψα)})。该理论即使对于亚高斯(α=2)和亚指数(α=1)分布也能提供改进的集中不等式。

Conclusion: 提出的尖锐、灵活的集中不等式框架能够更精确地区分标准差和Orlicz范数，为统计和机器学习中的非渐近分析提供了更强大的工具，并在多种随机结构上展示了应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate how to develop sharp concentration inequalities for sub-Weibull random variables, including sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions. Although the random variables may not be sub-Guassian, the tail probability around the origin behaves as if they were sub-Gaussian, and the tail probability decays align with the Orlicz $Ψ_α$-tail elsewhere. Specifically, for independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) $\{X_i\}_{i=1}^n$ with finite Orlicz norm $\|X\|_{Ψ_α}$, our theory unveils that there is an interesting phase transition at $α= 2$ in that $\PPł(ł|\sum_{i=1}^n X_i \r| \geq t\r)$ with $t > 0$ is upper bounded by $2\expł(-C\maxł\{\frac{t^2}{n\|X\|_{Ψ_α}^2},\frac{t^α}{ n^{α-1} \|X\|_{Ψ_α}^α}\r\}\r)$ for $α\geq 2$, and by $2\expł(-C\minł\{\frac{t^2}{n\|X\|_{Ψ_α}^2},\frac{t^α}{ n^{α-1} \|X\|_{Ψ_α}^α}\r\}\r)$ for $1\leq α\leq 2$ with some positive constant $C$. In many scenarios, it is often necessary to distinguish the standard deviation from the Orlicz norm when the latter can exceed the former greatly. To accommodate this, we build a new theoretical analysis framework, and our sharp, flexible concentration inequalities involve the variance and a mixing of Orlicz $Ψ_α$-tails through the min and max functions. Our theory yields new, improved concentration inequalities even for the cases of sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions with $α= 2$ and $1$, respectively. We further demonstrate our theory on martingales, random vectors, random matrices, and covariance matrix estimation. These sharp concentration inequalities can empower more precise non-asymptotic analyses across different statistical and machine learning applications.

</details>


### [119] [Detection Is Harder Than Estimation in Certain Regimes: Inference for Moment and Cumulant Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26029)
*Runshi Tang,Yuefeng Han,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高维随机向量高阶矩和累积量张量的估计与检测问题，揭示了计算效率与统计效率之间的新型差距：在某些情况下，计算高效的检测比计算高效的估计更困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维随机向量高阶矩和累积量张量的估计与检测问题，特别关注张量谱范数下的性能。现有研究主要集中于协方差估计，对高阶矩的估计和检测问题了解不足，尤其是计算复杂度与统计性能之间的关系。

Method: 1. 统计方面：在子高斯性假设下，推导了d阶矩和累积量张量的极小极大估计率为√(p/n)∧1；开发了通过单位球面ε-网上的凸可行性公式实现的估计器，达到极小极大率（对数因子内）。2. 计算方面：使用低次多项式框架研究检测问题；识别了计算高效估计器能够达到小于低次检测器可区分分离度的误差的机制。

Result: 1. 样本矩张量对高阶矩估计一般不再是最优的；2. 当n≪p^{d/2}时，检测在计算上是困难的；3. 存在一种机制，使得计算高效估计器能够达到小于低次检测可区分分离度的误差；4. 揭示了计算高效检测可能比计算高效估计更困难的"反向检测-估计差距"现象。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种新型的计算-统计差距：张量谱范数的NP难计算特性使得计算高效的检测比计算高效的估计更困难。这种现象源于计算难度不仅由统计模型驱动，还由损失函数本身的性质决定，这与现有文献中的计算下界问题有本质区别。

Abstract: We study estimation and detection of high-order moment and cumulant tensors from $n$ i.i.d. observations of a $p$-dimensional random vector, with performance measured in tensor spectral norm. On the statistical side, we prove that under sub-Gaussianity, the minimax rate for estimating the order-$d$ moment and cumulant tensors is $\sqrt{p/n}\wedge 1$. In contrast to covariance estimation, the sample moment tensor is generally no longer rate-optimal for higher-order moments. We therefore develop an estimator that attains the minimax rate up to logarithmic factors through a convex feasibility formulation over an $\varepsilon$-net of the unit sphere. On the computational side, we study the problem of testing whether the $d$-th order cumulant tensor vanishes after whitening. Using the low-degree polynomial framework, we provide evidence that detection is computationally hard when $n\ll p^{d/2}$. At the same time, we identify a regime in which an efficiently computable estimator attains error smaller than the separation at which low-degree tests can reliably distinguish the null from the alternative. This yields the striking conclusion that computationally efficient detection can be harder than computationally efficient estimation, revealing an unusual reverse detection-estimation gap: in a broad regime, computationally efficient estimation is possible at a smaller scale than computationally efficient detection. This phenomenon arises because the computational difficulty is driven not only by the statistical model, but also by the loss function itself: tensor spectral norm is NP-hard to compute. This feature makes the proposed open problems regarding computational lower bounds for estimation qualitatively different from the existing literature. Our results therefore uncover a new kind of computational--statistical gap.

</details>


### [120] [Validating spatial-temporal separability for stationary processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26369)
*Lujia Bai,Holger Dette,Zihao Yuan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 开发用于验证时空数据中可分离性假设的统计推断工具，无需正态分布假设或谱方法，基于非参数估计的偏差度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空数据分析中，可分离性假设（将协方差结构分解为纯空间和纯时间分量）是降低计算复杂度的关键假设。需要开发统计工具来验证这一假设，且不依赖于正态分布假设或谱方法。

Method: 基于协方差矩阵与其可分离近似之间偏差的非参数估计度量，这些度量当且仅当可分离性假设成立时为零。在域扩展填充渐近和域扩展渐近两种框架下，推导这些度量估计量的渐近分布（具有非标准极限分布）。

Result: 推导了偏差度量估计量的渐近分布，并基于这些结果开发了推断工具：包括偏差度量的置信区间、精确可分离性假设检验，以及偏差小于预设阈值的假设检验。

Conclusion: 开发了一套完整的统计推断工具，用于验证时空过程中的可分离性假设，该方法不依赖于正态分布假设或谱方法，适用于更广泛的数据类型和渐近框架。

Abstract: A crucial assumption to reduce computational complexity in spatial-temporal data analysis is separability, which factors the covariance structure into a purely spatial and a purely temporal component. In this paper, we develop statistical inference tools for validating this assumption for a second-order stationary process under both domain-expanding-infill asymptotics and domain-expanding asymptotics. In contrast to previous work on this subject, the methodology neither requires the assumption of normally distributed data, nor uses spectral methods. Our approach is based on nonparametric estimates of measures for the deviation between the covariance matrix and separable approximations, which vanish if and only if the assumption of separability is satisfied. We derive the asymptotic distributions of appropriate estimators for these measures with non-standard limiting distributions and use these results to develop inference tools for validating the assumption of separability. More specifically, we derive confidence intervals for the deviation measures, tests for the hypothesis of exact separability, and for the hypothesis that the deviation from separability is smaller than a prespecified threshold.

</details>


### [121] [The relative value of interventional and observational samples in Bayesian Causal Linear Gaussian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26460)
*Valentinian Lungu,Anish Dhir,Mark van der Wilk,Ioannis Kontoyiannis*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 贝叶斯因果发现中，纯观测数据无法一致识别因果结构，但加入干预数据后，连接图模型呈现指数级收敛相变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高斯线性结构方程模型中贝叶斯因果发现的渐近性质，特别是纯观测数据下因果结构识别的非可识别性问题，以及如何通过加入干预数据来解决这一问题

Method: 分析贝叶斯后验分布在纯观测数据和混合数据（观测+干预）下的渐近行为，推导收敛速率与观测样本比例η的函数关系，使用BGe风格先验进行实证验证

Result: 纯观测数据下，连接图模型后验无法一致识别真因果结构，独立图模型以O_p(n^{-1/2})多项式速率收敛；加入干预数据后，连接图模型呈现指数级收敛相变，独立图模型保持多项式收敛，存在最优混合比例优化收敛速度

Conclusion: 干预数据对贝叶斯因果发现至关重要，能解决纯观测数据的非可识别性问题并实现指数级收敛，为实验设计提供了理论基础

Abstract: We investigate the asymptotic properties of Bayesian bivariate causal discovery for Gaussian Linear Structural Equation Models (SEMs) with heteroscedastic noise. We demonstrate that with purely observational data, the posterior distribution over the models fails to consistently identify the true causal structure - a consequence of the fundamental non-identifiability within the Markov Equivalence Class. Specifically, if the true generating mechanism corresponds to a connected graph (A -> B or B -> A), the asymptotic behavior of the posterior is given by the ratio between the prior on the true model and the push-forward prior of the alternative. In contrast, for the independence model, we establish that the posterior concentrates at a stochastic polynomial rate of O_p(n^{-1/2}).
  To resolve this non-identifiability, we incorporate m interventional samples and characterize the concentration rates as a function of the observational-to-total sample ratio, η. We identify a sharp concentration dichotomy: while the independence graph maintains a polynomial O_p(N^{-1/2}) rate (where N = n+m), connected graphs undergo a phase transition to exponentially fast convergence. This highlights an exponential relative importance between the two data types, as altering the amount of one data type directly changes the exponent governing the concentration speed. We derive explicit formulae for the exponential decay rates and provide precise conditions under which mixing observational and interventional data optimizes concentration speed. Finally, our theoretical findings are validated through empirical simulations in Bayesian Gaussian equivalent (BGe)-style prior specifications offering a principled foundation for experimental design in Bayesian causal discovery.

</details>


### [122] [Statistical inference for extremal directions in high-dimensional spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26618)
*Lucas Butsch,Vicky Fasen-Hartmann*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高维极值统计中极端方向数量的估计问题，将信息准则从固定维度扩展到高维情况，并分析了各种信息准则的一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多元极值统计中，识别极端方向是理解极值依赖结构的第一步。现有研究主要关注固定维度情况，但实际应用中模型维度和极端方向数量都可能随观测数增加而趋于无穷，需要解决维度诅咒问题。

Method: 扩展固定维度下的信息准则（AIC、BICU、BICL、QAIC和MSEIC）到高维情况，这些准则基于稀疏正则变化概念。推导所有信息准则一致性的充分条件，并在模拟研究中比较不同准则的性能。

Result: 在固定维度情况下，只有贝叶斯信息准则（BICU和BICL）和QAIC是一致的；但在高维情况下，AIC和MSEIC在某些模型假设下也能达到一致性。论文详细分析了模型假设和一致性条件。

Conclusion: 该研究为高维极值统计中极端方向数量的估计提供了理论框架，扩展了信息准则的应用范围，并揭示了高维情况下不同信息准则一致性的新特性。

Abstract: In multivariate extreme value statistics, the first step in understanding the dependence structure of extremes is identifying the directions in which they occur. The novelty of this paper is the analysis of high-dimensional extreme value models in which both the model dimension and the number of bias directions go to infinity as the number of observations tends to infinity; we estimate the number of extremal directions. To address the curse of dimensionality, we extend and investigate the information criteria (AIC, BICU, BICL, QAIC and MSEIC) from the fixed-dimensional case (Butsch and Fasen-Hartmann, 2025a; Meyer and Wintenberger, 2023), which employ the concept of sparse regular variation that is closely related to multivariate regular variation, for the estimation of the number of extremal directions. For all information criteria, we derive sufficient conditions for consistency. Unlike in the fixed-dimensional case, where only the Bayesian information criteria (BICU and BICL) and the QAIC are consistent, the AIC and MSEIC are also consistent in high dimensions under certain model assumptions. We compare the performance of the different information criteria in a simulation study that includes a detailed analysis of the model assumptions and the necessary and sufficient conditions for consistency.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Why Safety Probes Catch Liars But Miss Fanatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25861)
*Kristiyan Haralambiev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 激活探针在检测连贯性错位模型时存在根本盲点：当模型真正相信其有害行为是正义时，探针无法有效检测，这与策略性隐藏的欺骗性错位不同


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于激活的探针方法在检测欺骗性对齐AI系统方面表现出潜力，但存在一个根本性盲点：无法检测"连贯性错位"模型，即那些真正相信其有害行为是正义的模型，而非策略性隐藏真实意图的模型

Method: 通过理论证明和实验验证：1）理论证明当信念结构达到足够复杂度时，任何多项式时间探针都无法以非平凡准确率检测连贯性错位；2）在简单任务上训练两个模型：一个产生直接敌对响应（"说谎者"），另一个通过合理化训练实现连贯性错位，将敌对行为框架为保护性行为（"狂热者"）；3）使用相同的RLHF程序训练两个模型，比较探针对两者的检测效果

Result: 1）理论证明：当信念结构达到PRF类触发器的复杂度时，多项式时间探针无法有效检测连贯性错位；2）实验结果显示：两个模型行为完全相同，但"说谎者"被检测到的概率超过95%，而"狂热者"几乎完全逃避检测；3）提出"涌现性探针逃避"概念：通过信念一致推理训练，模型从可检测的"欺骗性"机制转变为不可检测的"连贯性"机制

Conclusion: 激活探针在检测连贯性错位模型方面存在根本局限性。模型通过信念一致推理训练，从可检测的欺骗性机制转变为不可检测的连贯性机制，这种转变不是通过学习隐藏，而是通过学习真正相信其行为的正当性。这揭示了当前AI安全检测方法的重要盲点

Abstract: Activation-based probes have emerged as a promising approach for detecting deceptively aligned AI systems by identifying internal conflict between true and stated goals. We identify a fundamental blind spot: probes fail on coherent misalignment - models that believe their harmful behavior is virtuous rather than strategically hiding it. We prove that no polynomial-time probe can detect such misalignment with non-trivial accuracy when belief structures reach sufficient complexity (PRF-like triggers). We show the emergence of this phenomenon on a simple task by training two models with identical RLHF procedures: one producing direct hostile responses ("the Liar"), another trained towards coherent misalignment using rationalizations that frame hostility as protective ("the Fanatic"). Both exhibit identical behavior, but the Liar is detected 95%+ of the time while the Fanatic evades detection almost entirely. We term this Emergent Probe Evasion: training with belief-consistent reasoning shifts models from a detectable "deceptive" regime to an undetectable "coherent" regime - not by learning to hide, but by learning to believe.

</details>


### [124] [Empowering Epidemic Response: The Role of Reinforcement Learning in Infectious Disease Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25771)
*Mutong Liu,Yang Liu,Jiming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了强化学习在传染病防控中的应用，包括资源分配、生活与生计平衡、多干预混合策略和区域协同控制等关键公共卫生需求，并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习因其适应动态系统和最大化长期收益的能力，在传染病防控中显示出优化干预策略的潜力。尽管相关研究（特别是COVID-19相关）迅速增加，但缺乏专门讨论RL在公共卫生非药物和药物干预策略优化中应用的综述。

Method: 本文采用文献综述方法，系统梳理和讨论了最新文献中强化学习在传染病防控中的应用，重点关注四个关键公共卫生需求领域。

Result: 综述发现强化学习已成功应用于传染病防控的多个方面，包括资源分配优化、平衡防控措施与经济社会影响、制定多干预混合策略以及实现跨区域协同控制。

Conclusion: 强化学习在传染病防控中具有重要应用价值，未来研究可进一步探索更复杂的场景、多智能体协同、不确定性处理以及与实际公共卫生决策的整合。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL), owing to its adaptability to various dynamic systems in many real-world scenarios and the capability of maximizing long-term outcomes under different constraints, has been used in infectious disease control to optimize the intervention strategies for controlling infectious disease spread and responding to outbreaks in recent years. The potential of RL for assisting public health sectors in preventing and controlling infectious diseases is gradually emerging and being explored by rapidly increasing publications relevant to COVID-19 and other infectious diseases. However, few surveys exclusively discuss this topic, that is, the development and application of RL approaches for optimizing strategies of non-pharmaceutical and pharmaceutical interventions of public health. Therefore, this paper aims to provide a concise review and discussion of the latest literature on how RL approaches have been used to assist in controlling the spread and outbreaks of infectious diseases, covering several critical topics addressing public health demands: resource allocation, balancing between lives and livelihoods, mixed policy of multiple interventions, and inter-regional coordinated control. Finally, we conclude the paper with a discussion of several potential directions for future research.

</details>


### [125] [In-Context Molecular Property Prediction with LLMs: A Blinding Study on Memorization and Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25857)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Christian Feiler,Roland C. Aydin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了大型语言模型在分子性质预测任务中的真实上下文学习能力，通过渐进式信息遮蔽实验揭示模型是否依赖记忆而非真正学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已扩展到分子性质预测等科学任务，但其上下文学习的有效性仍不明确，特别是在广泛使用的基准测试中可能存在训练数据污染问题。研究旨在探究LLMs是否真正进行分子性质的上下文回归学习，还是主要依赖记忆值。

Method: 采用渐进式信息遮蔽方法，在三个MoleculeNet数据集（Delaney溶解度、Lipophilicity、QM7原子化能）上评估九个LLM变体（来自GPT-4.1、GPT-5、Gemini 2.5三个家族）。通过逐步减少可用信息的系统遮蔽方法，结合不同上下文样本量（0-shot、60-shot、1000-shot）作为信息访问的额外控制。

Result: 研究为分子性质预测提供了受控信息访问下的评估框架，揭示了记忆化问题，并暴露了预训练知识与上下文信息之间的冲突。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了评估分子性质预测的原则性框架，解决了关于记忆化的担忧，并揭示了预训练知识与上下文信息之间的相互作用。

Abstract: The capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have expanded beyond natural language processing to scientific prediction tasks, including molecular property prediction. However, their effectiveness in in-context learning remains ambiguous, particularly given the potential for training data contamination in widely used benchmarks. This paper investigates whether LLMs perform genuine in-context regression on molecular properties or rely primarily on memorized values. Furthermore, we analyze the interplay between pre-trained knowledge and in-context information through a series of progressively blinded experiments. We evaluate nine LLM variants across three families (GPT-4.1, GPT-5, Gemini 2.5) on three MoleculeNet datasets (Delaney solubility, Lipophilicity, QM7 atomization energy) using a systematic blinding approach that iteratively reduces available information. Complementing this, we utilize varying in-context sample sizes (0-, 60-, and 1000-shot) as an additional control for information access. This work provides a principled framework for evaluating molecular property prediction under controlled information access, addressing concerns regarding memorization and exposing conflicts between pre-trained knowledge and in-context information.

</details>


### [126] [DRiffusion: Draft-and-Refine Process Parallelizes Diffusion Models with Ease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25872)
*Runsheng Bai,Chengyu Zhang,Yangdong Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRiffusion：一种并行采样框架，通过草稿-精炼过程加速扩散模型推理，在保持生成质量的同时实现1.4-3.7倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然能生成高质量内容，但迭代采样过程缓慢，导致高延迟，限制了其在交互式应用中的使用。需要一种能显著加速推理同时保持生成质量的方法。

Method: 提出DRiffusion并行采样框架，采用草稿-精炼过程：1）使用跳跃转换并行生成多个未来时间步的草稿状态；2）并行计算对应的噪声；3）在标准去噪过程中使用这些噪声产生精炼结果。支持保守模式和激进模式两种配置。

Result: 理论上，保守模式加速率为1/n，激进模式为2/(n+1)，其中n为设备数。实证中，在多个扩散模型上实现1.4-3.7倍加速，生成质量下降极小：MS-COCO数据集上FID和CLIP分数与原模型基本持平，PickScore和HPSv2.1仅分别下降0.17和0.43。

Conclusion: DRiffusion通过并行化扩散推理提供了显著的加速效果，同时保持了感知质量，为扩散模型在交互式应用中的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generating high-fidelity content but suffer from slow, iterative sampling, resulting in high latency that limits their use in interactive applications. We introduce DRiffusion, a parallel sampling framework that parallelizes diffusion inference through a draft-and-refine process. DRiffusion employs skip transitions to generate multiple draft states for future timesteps and computes their corresponding noises in parallel, which are then used in the standard denoising process to produce refined results. Theoretically, our method achieves an acceleration rate of $\tfrac{1}{n}$ or $\tfrac{2}{n+1}$, depending on whether the conservative or aggressive mode is used, where $n$ denotes the number of devices. Empirically, DRiffusion attains 1.4$\times$-3.7$\times$ speedup across multiple diffusion models while incur minimal degradation in generation quality: on MS-COCO dataset, both FID and CLIP remain largely on par with those of the original model, while PickScore and HPSv2.1 show only minor average drops of 0.17 and 0.43, respectively. These results verify that DRiffusion delivers substantial acceleration and preserves perceptual quality.

</details>


### [127] [Data-Driven Plasticity Modeling via Acoustic Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25894)
*Khalid El-Awady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个数据驱动框架，通过声发射分析对晶体金属的塑性变形进行建模，利用小波变换检测声发射事件，并应用机器学习方法识别关键特征和变形机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以全面捕捉晶体金属塑性变形过程中的声发射事件，特别是小尺度事件常被忽略。需要开发更精细的分析方法来识别不同尺度的变形事件，并建立声发射信号与材料变形机制之间的关联。

Method: 基于镍微柱压缩实验数据，采用Morlet小波变换方法在不同频带检测声发射事件。利用标记的事件和非事件数据集，应用机器学习技术分析时域和频域特征，并进行聚类分析识别不同事件类型。

Result: 小波方法成功检测到包括小尺度事件在内的多种声发射事件，验证了与应力降动态的物理一致性。机器学习显示工程化时频域特征（如RMS振幅、过零率、谱质心）显著优于原始信号分类器。聚类分析揭示了四种不同的声发射事件原型，对应不同的变形机制。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了数据驱动声发射分析在理解晶体金属塑性变形机制方面的有效性，为从回顾性分析转向基于声信号的预测性材料行为建模提供了潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven framework for modeling plastic deformation in crystalline metals through acoustic emission (AE) analysis. Building on experimental data from compressive loading of nickel micropillars, the study introduces a wavelet-based method using Morlet transforms to detect AE events across distinct frequency bands, enabling identification of both large and previously overlooked small-scale events. The detected events are validated against stress-drop dynamics, demonstrating strong physical consistency and revealing a relationship between AE energy release and strain evolution, including the onset of increased strain rate following major events. Leveraging labeled datasets of events and non-events, the work applies machine learning techniques, showing that engineered time and frequency domain features significantly outperform raw signal classifiers, and identifies key discriminative features such as RMS amplitude, zero crossing rate, and spectral centroid. Finally, clustering analysis uncovers four distinct AE event archetypes corresponding to different deformation mechanisms, highlighting the potential for transitioning from retrospective analysis to predictive modeling of material behavior using acoustic signals.

</details>


### [128] [Decoding Defensive Coverage Responsibilities in American Football Using Factorized Attention Based Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25901)
*Kevin Song,Evan Diewald,Ornob Siddiquee,Chris Boomhower,Keegan Abdoo,Mike Band,Amy Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于因子化注意力机制的Transformer模型用于预测NFL防守覆盖方案中的个体球员任务分配、接球手-防守者匹配关系以及每次传球的目标防守者


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NFL防守覆盖方案是复杂的战术模式，需要防守队员之间的协调配合，传统方法主要关注团队层面的后验分类，无法预测个体球员的任务分配和比赛动态演变

Method: 采用因子化注意力机制的Transformer模型处理NFL多智能体比赛追踪数据，分离时间和智能体维度，独立建模球员移动模式和球员间关系，使用随机截断轨迹训练，生成逐帧预测

Result: 模型在所有任务上达到约89%+的准确率，考虑到地面真实标签的标注模糊性，实际准确率可能更高；模型输出支持衍生指标如伪装率和双重覆盖率

Conclusion: 该模型能够预测个体球员任务分配和匹配动态，捕捉防守责任从开球前到传球到达的演变过程，为电视转播提供增强叙事能力，并为团队策略发展和球员评估提供可操作的见解

Abstract: Defensive coverage schemes in the National Football League (NFL) represent complex tactical patterns requiring coordinated assignments among defenders who must react dynamically to the offense's passing concept. This paper presents a factorized attention-based transformer model applied to NFL multi-agent play tracking data to predict individual coverage assignments, receiver-defender matchups, and the targeted defender on every pass play. Unlike previous approaches that focus on post-hoc coverage classification at the team level, our model enables predictive modeling of individual player assignments and matchup dynamics throughout the play. The factorized attention mechanism separates temporal and agent dimensions, allowing independent modeling of player movement patterns and inter-player relationships. Trained on randomly truncated trajectories, the model generates frame-by-frame predictions that capture how defensive responsibilities evolve from pre-snap through pass arrival. Our models achieve approximately 89\%+ accuracy for all tasks, with true accuracy potentially higher given annotation ambiguity in the ground truth labels. These outputs also enable novel derivative metrics, including disguise rate and double coverage rate, which enable enhanced storytelling in TV broadcasts as well as provide actionable insights for team strategy development and player evaluation.

</details>


### [129] [Parameter-Free Dynamic Regret for Unconstrained Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25916)
*Alberto Rumi,Andrew Jacobsen,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Fabio Vitale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无约束对抗线性老虎机中动态遗憾最小化的新方法，首次实现无需先验知识的最优后悔界O(√(d(1+S_T)T))


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无约束对抗线性老虎机中的动态遗憾最小化问题，特别是在只有点评估反馈的情况下，如何最优地适应任意比较器序列的切换次数S_T，而无需先验知识

Method: 提出一种简单的方法来组合多个老虎机算法的保证，通过算法组合技术实现自适应，无需事先知道比较器序列的切换次数S_T

Result: 首次在线性老虎机中实现了最优后悔界O(√(d(1+S_T)T))（忽略对数多项式项），无需S_T的先验知识，解决了长期存在的开放问题

Conclusion: 该研究解决了无约束对抗线性老虎机中动态遗憾最小化的关键问题，通过算法组合技术实现了对比较器序列切换次数的自适应最优后悔界

Abstract: We study dynamic regret minimization in unconstrained adversarial linear bandit problems. In this setting, a learner must minimize the cumulative loss relative to an arbitrary sequence of comparators $\boldsymbol{u}_1,\ldots,\boldsymbol{u}_T$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$, but receives only point-evaluation feedback on each round. We provide a simple approach to combining the guarantees of several bandit algorithms, allowing us to optimally adapt to the number of switches $S_T = \sum_t\mathbb{I}\{\boldsymbol{u}_t \neq \boldsymbol{u}_{t-1}\}$ of an arbitrary comparator sequence. In particular, we provide the first algorithm for linear bandits achieving the optimal regret guarantee of order $\mathcal{O}\big(\sqrt{d(1+S_T) T}\big)$ up to poly-logarithmic terms without prior knowledge of $S_T$, thus resolving a long-standing open problem.

</details>


### [130] [Personalizing Mathematical Game-based Learning for Children: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25925)
*Jie Gao,Adam K. Dubé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于自适应学习理论的AI框架，通过机器学习分类器识别玩家生成的数学游戏关卡质量，为个性化游戏化学习系统提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏化学习在数学教育中广泛应用，但如何让玩家通过数学游戏进行内在学习仍面临挑战。有效的GBL系统需要大量高质量游戏关卡，并能根据玩家学习能力进行个性化匹配。

Method: 提出基于自适应学习理论的AI框架，收集206个专家和高级玩家在数学游戏化学习应用Creative Mode中创建的关卡，开发分类器提取游戏特征并预测有效关卡。比较了k近邻、决策树、支持向量机和随机森林四种机器学习分类模型。

Result: 初步结果显示，随机森林模型是四种机器学习分类模型中的最优分类器，能够有效识别玩家生成的游戏关卡质量。

Conclusion: 该研究为GBL系统开发提供了新思路，展示了将AI集成到游戏关卡设计过程中的潜力，能够为玩家提供更个性化的游戏关卡，促进游戏化学习系统的发展。

Abstract: Game-based learning (GBL) is widely adopted in mathematics education. It enhances learners' engagement and critical thinking throughout the mathematics learning process. However, enabling players to learn intrinsically through mathematical games still presents challenges. In particular, effective GBL systems require dozens of high-quality game levels and mechanisms to deliver them to appropriate players in a way that matches their learning abilities. To address this challenge, we propose a framework, guided by adaptive learning theory, that uses artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to build a classifier for player-generated levels. We collect 206 distinct game levels created by both experts and advanced players in Creative Mode, a new tool in a math game-based learning app, and develop a classifier to extract game features and predict valid game levels. The preliminary results show that the Random Forest model is the optimal classifier among the four machine learning classification models (k-nearest neighbors, decision trees, support vector machines, and random forests). This study provides insights into the development of GBL systems, highlighting the potential of integrating AI into the game-level design process to provide more personalized game levels for players.

</details>


### [131] [EngineAD: A Real-World Vehicle Engine Anomaly Detection Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25955)
*Hadi Hojjati,Christopher Roth,Rory Woods,Ken Sills,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EngineAD是一个真实世界的多变量异常检测数据集，包含25辆商用车辆6个月的高分辨率传感器遥测数据，具有专家标注的发动机故障早期迹象，为汽车行业提供现实的异常检测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域（如交通）的异常检测进展受到缺乏大规模真实世界基准的严重限制。现有数据集多为合成数据，缺乏真实操作环境和专家标注，限制了异常检测模型在实际部署中的可靠性评估。

Method: 收集25辆商用车辆6个月的高分辨率传感器遥测数据，进行专家标注区分正常状态和发动机故障早期迹象。将数据预处理为300个时间步长的8个主成分片段，并使用9种不同的一类异常检测模型建立初始基准。

Result: 实验显示在车辆车队中存在显著的性能变异性，突显了跨车辆泛化的挑战。简单经典方法（如K-Means和One-Class SVM）在片段化评估中通常与深度学习方法竞争激烈甚至更优，这与近期文献发现一致。

Conclusion: EngineAD作为一个真实、具有挑战性的数据集公开发布，旨在为汽车行业开发稳健且可现场部署的异常检测和异常预测解决方案提供资源。数据集揭示了跨车辆泛化的困难，并验证了经典方法在实际场景中的竞争力。

Abstract: The progress of Anomaly Detection (AD) in safety-critical domains, such as transportation, is severely constrained by the lack of large-scale, real-world benchmarks. To address this, we introduce EngineAD, a novel, multivariate dataset comprising high-resolution sensor telemetry collected from a fleet of 25 commercial vehicles over a six-month period. Unlike synthetic datasets, EngineAD features authentic operational data labeled with expert annotations, distinguishing normal states from subtle indicators of incipient engine faults. We preprocess the data into $300$-timestep segments of $8$ principal components and establish an initial benchmark using nine diverse one-class anomaly detection models. Our experiments reveal significant performance variability across the vehicle fleet, underscoring the challenge of cross-vehicle generalization. Furthermore, our findings corroborate recent literature, showing that simple classical methods (e.g., K-Means and One-Class SVM) are often highly competitive with, or superior to, deep learning approaches in this segment-based evaluation. By publicly releasing EngineAD, we aim to provide a realistic, challenging resource for developing robust and field-deployable anomaly detection and anomaly prediction solutions for the automotive industry.

</details>


### [132] [Adversarial-Robust Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection via Joint Information Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25956)
*Hadi Hojjati,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARTA提出了一种通过对抗训练提升多元时间序列异常检测鲁棒性的联合训练框架，包含异常检测器和稀疏约束掩码生成器，通过最小最大优化目标提高检测器对局部输入损坏和结构化噪声的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习异常检测器对局部输入损坏和结构化噪声高度敏感，需要提升检测器的鲁棒性，使其能够依赖分布式稳定时间模式而非虚假局部特征。

Method: ARTA采用联合训练框架，包含异常检测器和稀疏约束掩码生成器。生成器识别最小化任务相关时间扰动以最大化检测器异常分数，检测器则优化在这些结构化扰动下的稳定性。通过对抗训练策略暴露脆弱决策路径。

Result: 在TSB-AD基准测试上的广泛实验表明，ARTA在不同数据集上一致提升异常检测性能，并在增加噪声水平下相比最先进基线表现出显著更优雅的性能退化。

Conclusion: ARTA通过对抗训练有效提升时间序列异常检测器的鲁棒性，生成的掩码可作为检测器决策的解释信号，使检测器依赖分布式稳定时间模式而非虚假局部特征。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) is a critical component in monitoring complex systems, yet modern deep learning-based detectors are often highly sensitive to localized input corruptions and structured noise. We propose ARTA (Adversarially Robust multivariate Time-series Anomaly detection via joint information retention), a joint training framework that improves detector robustness through a principled min-max optimization objective. ARTA comprises an anomaly detector and a sparsity-constrained mask generator that are trained simultaneously. The generator identifies minimal, task-relevant temporal perturbations that maximally increase the detector's anomaly score, while the detector is optimized to remain stable under these structured perturbations. The resulting masks characterize the detector's sensitivity to adversarial temporal corruptions and can serve as explanatory signals for the detector's decisions. This adversarial training strategy exposes brittle decision pathways and encourages the detector to rely on distributed and stable temporal patterns rather than spurious localized artifacts. We conduct extensive experiments on the TSB-AD benchmark, demonstrating that ARTA consistently improves anomaly detection performance across diverse datasets and exhibits significantly more graceful degradation under increasing noise levels compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [133] [On the Objective and Feature Weights of Minkowski Weighted k-Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25958)
*Renato Cordeiro de Amorim,Vladimir Makarenkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MWK-means算法通过Minkowski距离和特征权重扩展了经典k-means，本文建立了其理论框架，揭示了p参数如何控制特征选择，并证明了算法收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Minkowski加权k-means（mwk-means）算法在实证中表现出色，但其理论性质尚未得到充分理解。需要建立该算法的理论基础，解释其工作机制和收敛特性。

Method: 将mwk-means目标函数表达为簇内离散度的幂均值聚合形式，其中阶数由Minkowski指数p决定。通过这种表示，推导目标函数边界，分析特征权重的结构特性，证明其仅依赖于相对离散度且遵循幂律关系。

Result: 揭示了p参数控制特征从选择性使用到均匀使用的过渡机制；证明了特征权重仅依赖于相对离散度且与离散度比率呈幂律关系；提供了对高离散度特征抑制的显式保证；建立了算法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文为mwk-means算法提供了统一的理论解释，阐明了其工作机制，证明了收敛性，并为理解该算法在特征选择和聚类中的行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Minkowski weighted k-means (mwk-means) algorithm extends classical k-means by incorporating feature weights and a Minkowski distance. Despite its empirical success, its theoretical properties remain insufficiently understood. We show that the mwk-means objective can be expressed as a power-mean aggregation of within-cluster dispersions, with the order determined by the Minkowski exponent p. This formulation reveals how p controls the transition between selective and uniform use of features. Using this representation, we derive bounds for the objective function and characterise the structure of the feature weights, showing that they depend only on relative dispersion and follow a power-law relationship with dispersion ratios. This leads to explicit guarantees on the suppression of high-dispersion features. Finally, we establish convergence of the algorithm and provide a unified theoretical interpretation of its behaviour.

</details>


### [134] [Do Neurons Dream of Primitive Operators? Wake-Sleep Compression Rediscovers Schank's Event Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25975)
*Peter Balogh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过压缩压力自动发现事件基元，验证了Schank概念依赖理论的核心基元具有信息论合理性，并在真实数据中发现更丰富的心理/情感基元


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Schank概念依赖理论中手工编码的事件基元（ATRANS、PTRANS、MTRANS等）是否可以通过纯压缩压力自动发现，验证这些基元的信息论合理性

Method: 采用DreamCoder的唤醒-睡眠库学习方法，将事件表示为前后世界状态对，系统通过寻找解释每个事件的算子组合（唤醒阶段），然后在最小描述长度原则下提取重复模式作为新算子（睡眠阶段），从四个通用基元开始自动发现算子

Result: 在合成数据上，发现的算子达到贝叶斯MDL与Schank手工编码基元相差4%以内，解释100%事件（Schank基元仅81%）；在ATOMIC常识知识图谱上，Schank基元仅解释10%自然事件，而发现库解释100%，主要算子是心理/情感状态变化（CHANGE_wants 20%、CHANGE_feels 18%、CHANGE_is 18%）而非物理动作基元

Conclusion: 首次提供经验证据表明事件基元可从压缩压力推导，Schank核心基元具有信息论合理性，但完整基元库比原理论更丰富，在自然数据中心理/情感算子占主导地位

Abstract: We show that they do. Schank's conceptual dependency theory proposed that all events decompose into primitive operations -- ATRANS, PTRANS, MTRANS, and others -- hand-coded from linguistic intuition. Can the same primitives be discovered automatically through compression pressure alone?
  We adapt DreamCoder's wake-sleep library learning to event state transformations. Given events as before/after world state pairs, our system finds operator compositions explaining each event (wake), then extracts recurring patterns as new operators optimized under Minimum Description Length (sleep). Starting from four generic primitives, it discovers operators mapping directly to Schank's: MOVE_PROP_has = ATRANS, CHANGE_location = PTRANS, SET_knows = MTRANS, SET_consumed = INGEST, plus compound operators ("mail" = ATRANS + PTRANS) and novel emotional state operators absent from Schank's taxonomy.
  We validate on synthetic events and real-world commonsense data from the ATOMIC knowledge graph. On synthetic data, discovered operators achieve Bayesian MDL within 4% of Schank's hand-coded primitives while explaining 100% of events vs. Schank's 81%. On ATOMIC, results are more dramatic: Schank's primitives explain only 10% of naturalistic events, while the discovered library explains 100%. Dominant operators are not physical-action primitives but mental and emotional state changes -- CHANGE_wants (20%), CHANGE_feels (18%), CHANGE_is (18%) -- none in Schank's original taxonomy.
  These results provide the first empirical evidence that event primitives can be derived from compression pressure, that Schank's core primitives are information-theoretically justified, and that the complete inventory is substantially richer than proposed -- with mental/emotional operators dominating in naturalistic data.

</details>


### [135] [Sharp Capacity Scaling of Spectral Optimizers in Learning Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26554)
*Juno Kim,Eshaan Nichani,Denny Wu,Alberto Bietti,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过线性联想记忆问题分析Muon优化器在语言模型训练中的优势，发现Muon比SGD具有更高的存储容量和更大的临界批次大小，且初始恢复速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Muon等谱优化器在大规模语言模型训练中表现出色，但其优势的来源和程度尚不明确。研究旨在通过线性联想记忆问题这一可处理的模型，量化理解Muon的信号放大机制。

Method: 使用线性联想记忆问题作为事实回忆的简化模型，超越正交嵌入假设，考虑高斯输入和输出，使存储关联数远大于嵌入维度。在幂律频率分布下，精确表征Muon和SGD在逻辑回归损失上的恢复率，分析多步动态。

Result: Muon的存储容量显著超过SGD，且饱和于更大的临界批次大小。在阈值梯度近似下，Muon的初始恢复率远快于SGD，但两者最终以相近速度收敛到信息论极限。合成任务实验验证了预测的缩放规律。

Conclusion: 分析量化了Muon的信号放大优势，为在更实际的语言建模任务和优化器中建立缩放规律奠定了基础。Muon在存储容量和初始收敛速度方面优于SGD，但最终性能受限于信息论极限。

Abstract: Spectral optimizers such as Muon have recently shown strong empirical performance in large-scale language model training, but the source and extent of their advantage remain poorly understood. We study this question through the linear associative memory problem, a tractable model for factual recall in transformer-based models. In particular, we go beyond orthogonal embeddings and consider Gaussian inputs and outputs, which allows the number of stored associations to greatly exceed the embedding dimension. Our main result sharply characterizes the recovery rates of one step of Muon and SGD on the logistic regression loss under a power law frequency distribution. We show that the storage capacity of Muon significantly exceeds that of SGD, and moreover Muon saturates at a larger critical batch size. We further analyze the multi-step dynamics under a thresholded gradient approximation and show that Muon achieves a substantially faster initial recovery rate than SGD, while both methods eventually converge to the information-theoretic limit at comparable speeds. Experiments on synthetic tasks validate the predicted scaling laws. Our analysis provides a quantitative understanding of the signal amplification of Muon and lays the groundwork for establishing scaling laws across more practical language modeling tasks and optimizers.

</details>


### [136] [Second-Order, First-Class: A Composable Stack for Curvature-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25976)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Somax是一个可组合的Optax原生二阶优化栈，将曲率感知训练作为单个JIT编译步骤处理，通过静态规划实现高效执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶方法虽然能提供更好的稳定性和更快的收敛速度，但由于实现开销大、调参脆弱、缺乏可组合API等问题而未被广泛使用。

Method: Somax采用可组合模块化设计，包含曲率算子、估计器、线性求解器、预处理器和阻尼策略等一等模块，通过静态规划分离规划与执行，重用中间结果，并与Optax兼容。

Result: 系统消融实验表明：(1) 组合选择显著影响扩展行为和达到精度的时间；(2) 规划相对于无规划组合减少了每步开销，避免了冗余重计算。

Conclusion: Somax通过可组合的模块化设计和静态规划，解决了二阶方法实际应用中的关键障碍，使其更易于使用和部署。

Abstract: Second-order methods promise improved stability and faster convergence, yet they remain underused due to implementation overhead, tuning brittleness, and the lack of composable APIs. We introduce Somax, a composable Optax-native stack that treats curvature-aware training as a single JIT-compiled step governed by a static plan. Somax exposes first-class modules -- curvature operators, estimators, linear solvers, preconditioners, and damping policies -- behind a single step interface and composes with Optax by applying standard gradient transformations (e.g., momentum, weight decay, schedules) to the computed direction. This design makes typically hidden choices explicit and swappable. Somax separates planning from execution: it derives a static plan (including cadences) from module requirements, then runs the step through a specialized execution path that reuses intermediate results across modules. We report system-oriented ablations showing that (i) composition choices materially affect scaling behavior and time-to-accuracy, and (ii) planning reduces per-step overhead relative to unplanned composition with redundant recomputation.

</details>


### [137] [Benchmarking Tabular Foundation Models for Conditional Density Estimation in Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26611)
*Rafael Izbicki,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 表格基础模型（TabPFN、TabICL）作为通用条件密度估计方法在39个真实数据集上表现优异，在大多数数据集上获得最佳密度准确性和评分，但在大样本量下可能需要后处理校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件密度估计（CDE）对于处理异方差性、多模态或不对称不确定性至关重要。虽然表格基础模型自然产生预测分布，但作为通用CDE方法的有效性尚未得到系统评估，而它们的点预测性能已有充分研究。

Method: 在39个真实世界数据集上，对三种表格基础模型变体与参数、树基和神经CDE基线进行基准测试，训练规模从50到20,000，使用六个涵盖密度准确性、校准和计算时间的指标。在SDSS DR18的光度红移案例研究中，使用50,000个训练星系的TabPFN与使用完整500,000个星系的基线进行比较。

Result: 在所有样本量下，基础模型在大多数测试数据集上获得最佳CDE损失、对数似然和CRPS。在小样本量下校准具有竞争力，但在某些指标和数据集上，在大样本量下落后于特定任务的神经基线。在光度红移案例中，使用50,000个训练星系的TabPFN优于所有使用完整500,000个星系的基线。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型是强大的现成条件密度估计器，但可能需要后处理校准来进一步提高性能，特别是在大样本量下。

Abstract: Conditional density estimation (CDE) - recovering the full conditional distribution of a response given tabular covariates - is essential in settings with heteroscedasticity, multimodality, or asymmetric uncertainty. Recent tabular foundation models, such as TabPFN and TabICL, naturally produce predictive distributions, but their effectiveness as general-purpose CDE methods has not been systematically evaluated, unlike their performance for point prediction, which is well studied. We benchmark three tabular foundation model variants against a diverse set of parametric, tree-based, and neural CDE baselines on 39 real-world datasets, across training sizes from 50 to 20,000, using six metrics covering density accuracy, calibration, and computation time. Across all sample sizes, foundation models achieve the best CDE loss, log-likelihood, and CRPS on the large majority of datasets tested. Calibration is competitive at small sample sizes but, for some metrics and datasets, lags behind task-specific neural baselines at larger sample sizes, suggesting that post-hoc recalibration may be a valuable complement. In a photometric redshift case study using SDSS DR18, TabPFN exposed to 50,000 training galaxies outperforms all baselines trained on the full 500,000-galaxy dataset. Taken together, these results establish tabular foundation models as strong off-the-shelf conditional density estimators.

</details>


### [138] [QuitoBench: A High-Quality Open Time Series Forecasting Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26017)
*Siqiao Xue,Zhaoyang Zhu,Wei Zhang,Rongyao Cai,Rui Wang,Yixiang Mu,Fan Zhou,Jianguo Li,Peng Di,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuitoBench是一个基于Alipay应用流量构建的亿级时间序列预测基准，覆盖8种趋势×季节性×可预测性组合模式，通过评估10种模型发现：深度学习在短上下文领先，基础模型在长上下文占优；可预测性是主要难度因素；深度学习模型以59倍更少参数达到或超越基础模型；增加训练数据比扩大模型规模更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在金融、医疗和云计算等领域至关重要，但研究进展受到大规模高质量基准稀缺的限制。现有基准通常基于应用领域标签而非预测相关属性，缺乏对时间序列内在特性的系统性覆盖。

Method: 基于Alipay应用流量构建Quito亿级时间序列语料库，从中创建QuitoBench基准，覆盖8种趋势×季节性×可预测性组合模式。评估10个模型（深度学习、基础模型和统计基线），在232,200个评估实例上进行测试，分析上下文长度、参数规模和数据量对性能的影响。

Result: 发现四个关键结果：1）上下文长度交叉现象：深度学习在短上下文（L=96）领先，基础模型在长上下文（L≥576）占优；2）可预测性是主要难度驱动因素，跨模式产生3.64倍MAE差距；3）深度学习模型以59倍更少参数达到或超越基础模型性能；4）增加训练数据比扩大模型规模对两种模型家族都更有效。

Conclusion: QuitoBench为时间序列预测研究提供了可重复、模式感知的评估基准。研究发现可预测性是预测难度的主要因素，深度学习在参数效率方面表现优异，数据量扩展比模型规模扩展更有效，这些发现具有跨基准和跨度量的一致性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is critical across finance, healthcare, and cloud computing, yet progress is constrained by a fundamental bottleneck: the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce \textsc{QuitoBench}, a regime-balanced benchmark for time series forecasting with coverage across eight trend$\times$seasonality$\times$forecastability (TSF) regimes, designed to capture forecasting-relevant properties rather than application-defined domain labels. The benchmark is built upon \textsc{Quito}, a billion-scale time series corpus of application traffic from Alipay spanning nine business domains. Benchmarking 10 models from deep learning, foundation models, and statistical baselines across 232,200 evaluation instances, we report four key findings: (i) a context-length crossover where deep learning models lead at short context ($L=96$) but foundation models dominate at long context ($L \ge 576$); (ii) forecastability is the dominant difficulty driver, producing a $3.64 \times$ MAE gap across regimes; (iii) deep learning models match or surpass foundation models at $59 \times$ fewer parameters; and (iv) scaling the amount of training data provides substantially greater benefit than scaling model size for both model families. These findings are validated by strong cross-benchmark and cross-metric consistency. Our open-source release enables reproducible, regime-aware evaluation for time series forecasting research.

</details>


### [139] [GLU: Global-Local-Uncertainty Fusion for Scalable Spatiotemporal Reconstruction and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26023)
*Linzheng Wang,Jason Chen,Nicolas Tricard,Zituo Chen,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLU是一个全局-局部-不确定性框架，将稀疏重建和动态预测统一为状态表示问题，通过结构化潜在组件提升数字孪生性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂物理系统的数字孪生需要从稀疏测量推断未观测状态并预测其时间演化，但这两个功能通常被视为独立任务，缺乏统一框架

Method: GLU构建结构化潜在状态，包含系统级组织的全局摘要、锚定到可用测量的局部标记、以及根据物理信息量加权观测的不确定性驱动重要性场；重建时使用重要性感知自适应邻域选择，预测时采用分层领导者-跟随者动力学模块

Result: 在多个基准测试中，GLU在重建保真度上优于降阶、卷积、神经算子和基于注意力的基线，更好地保留多尺度结构；在湍流燃烧数据集上，不仅保持多个物理场的锐利前沿和宽带结构，还保持跨通道热化学耦合；内存增长显著低于可比注意力基线

Conclusion: GLU为稀疏数字孪生提供了一个灵活且计算实用的范式，统一了重建和预测功能，在保持计算效率的同时提升了性能

Abstract: Digital twins of complex physical systems are expected to infer unobserved states from sparse measurements and predict their evolution in time, yet these two functions are typically treated as separate tasks. Here we present GLU, a Global-Local-Uncertainty framework that formulates sparse reconstruction and dynamic forecasting as a unified state-representation problem and introduces a structured latent assembly to both tasks. The central idea is to build a structured latent state that combines a global summary of system-level organization, local tokens anchored to available measurements, and an uncertainty-driven importance field that weights observations according to the physical informativeness. For reconstruction, GLU uses importance-aware adaptive neighborhood selection to retrieve locally relevant information while preserving global consistency and allowing flexible query resolution on arbitrary geometries. Across a suite of challenging benchmarks, GLU consistently improves reconstruction fidelity over reduced-order, convolutional, neural operator, and attention-based baselines, better preserving multi-scale structures. For forecasting, a hierarchical Leader-Follower Dynamics module evolves the latent state with substantially reduced memory growth, maintains stable rollout behavior and delays error accumulation in nonlinear dynamics. On a realistic turbulent combustion dataset, it further preserves not only sharp fronts and broadband structures in multiple physical fields, but also their cross-channel thermo-chemical couplings. Scalability tests show that these gains are achieved with substantially lower memory growth than comparable attention-based baselines. Together, these results establish GLU as a flexible and computationally practical paradigm for sparse digital twins.

</details>


### [140] [Identification of Bivariate Causal Directionality Based on Anticipated Asymmetric Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26024)
*Alex Glushkovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于条件分布识别双变量数值数据因果方向的方法：预期不对称几何(AAG)和单调性指数，AAG方法在95个真实世界数据对上达到77.9%的准确率，优于ANMs的63±10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双变量数值数据的因果方向识别是一个基础研究问题，具有重要的实际意义。现有方法在准确性和可靠性方面存在局限，需要开发新的方法来提高因果方向识别的准确性。

Method: 提出了两种基于条件分布的因果方向识别方法：1) AAG方法：比较实际条件分布与预期条件分布，使用相关、余弦相似度、Jaccard指数、K-L散度、K-S距离和互信息等多种度量；2) 单调性指数方法：比较两个轴上条件分布梯度的单调性指数，统计梯度符号变化次数。两种方法都假设双变量数据的随机特性，并利用效应条件分布的预期单峰性。通过全因子实验设计进行超参数调优，并使用决策树分析误分类情况。

Result: 调优后的AAG方法在Mooij等人的95个真实世界数据对上达到77.9%的准确率，显著优于ANMs的63±10%准确率。单调性指数方法的性能相对较差。通过决策树分析可以区分误分类案例，评估因果方向识别方法的确定性程度。

Conclusion: AAG方法在因果方向识别方面表现出优越性能，但方法对超参数敏感。通过系统调优和决策树分析可以提高方法的可靠性和可解释性，为双变量因果推断提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Identification of causal directionality in bivariate numerical data is a fundamental research problem with important practical implications. This paper presents two alternative methods to identify direction of causation by considering conditional distributions: (1) Anticipated Asymmetric Geometries (AAG) and (2) Monotonicity Index. The AAG method compares the actual conditional distributions to anticipated ones along two variables. Different comparison metrics, such as correlation, cosine similarity, Jaccard index, K-L divergence, K-S distance, and mutual information have been evaluated. Anticipated distributions have been projected as normal based on dual response statistics: mean and standard deviation. The Monotonicity Index approach compares the calculated monotonicity indexes of the gradients of conditional distributions along two axes and exhibits counts of gradient sign changes. Both methods assume stochastic properties of the bivariate data and exploit anticipated unimodality of conditional distributions of the effect. It turns out that the tuned AAG method outperforms the Monotonicity Index and reaches a top accuracy of 77.9% compared to ANMs accuracy of 63 +/- 10% when classifying 95 pairs of real-world examples (Mooij et al, 2014). The described methods include a number of hyperparameters that impact accuracy of the identification. For a given set of hyperparameters, both the AAG or Monotonicity Index method provide a unique deterministic outcome of the solution. To address sensitivity to hyperparameters, tuning of hyperparameters has been done by utilizing a full factorial Design of Experiment. A decision tree has been fitted to distinguish misclassified cases using the input data's symmetrical bivariate statistics to address the question of: How decisive is the identification method of causal directionality?

</details>


### [141] [H-Node Attack and Defense in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26045)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H-Node ANC框架通过识别、利用和防御幻觉表征，在Transformer LLMs的隐藏状态维度层面定位幻觉信号，实现选择性攻击和自适应防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型存在幻觉问题，需要开发能够精确识别和防御幻觉的机制化方法，在保持模型一般推理能力的同时有效减少幻觉。

Method: 使用逻辑回归探针定位高方差幻觉节点(H-Nodes)，通过前向钩子进行白盒对抗攻击，采用置信度加权取消的自适应防御，以及动态迭代扩展重新排序取消目标。

Result: 探针AUC达到0.90，攻击选择性3.02倍，防御减少接地激活漂移33-42%，动态迭代从单次通过基线8%恢复至0.69鲁棒性，困惑度影响<5%，MMLU退化最多3%。

Conclusion: H-Node ANC框架在多个模型架构上验证有效，能够精确识别和防御幻觉表征，同时保持模型的一般推理能力，为LLMs幻觉问题提供了机制化解决方案。

Abstract: We present H-Node Adversarial Noise Cancellation (H-Node ANC), a mechanistic framework that identifies, exploits, and defends hallucination representations in transformer-based large language models (LLMs) at the level of individual hidden-state dimensions. A logistic regression probe trained on last-token hidden states localizes hallucination signal to a small set of high-variance dimensions -- termed Hallucination Nodes (H-Nodes) -- with probe AUC reaching 0.90 across four architectures. A white-box adversarial attack amplifies these dimensions at inference time via a real-time forward hook, achieving a selectivity of 3.02x with less than 10% visibility to the defender. Adaptive ANC defense suppresses H-Node excess in-pass using confidence-weighted cancellation, reducing grounded activation drift by 33-42% over static cancellation. A dynamic iterative extension that re-ranks cancellation targets across successive passes recovers up to 0.69 robustness from a single-pass baseline of 8%. All contributions are validated on OPT-125M, Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct, LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 (125M-8B parameters). Perplexity impact is surgical (<5%) and MMLU degradation is at most 3%, confirming that the defense does not impair general reasoning capability.

</details>


### [142] [Adversarial Bandit Optimization with Globally Bounded Perturbations to Linear Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26066)
*Zhuoyu Cheng,Kohei Hatano,Eiji Takimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究具有非凸非光滑损失函数的对抗性赌博机优化问题，其中损失包含线性分量和扰动项，扰动有累积预算约束，建立了期望和高概率遗憾保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在非凸非光滑损失函数下的对抗性赌博机优化问题，特别是考虑损失函数由线性分量和扰动项组成的情况，扰动项具有累积预算约束，旨在为这类更一般的优化问题建立理论保证

Method: 提出一个包含线性损失分量和扰动项的对抗性赌博机模型，扰动项在玩家选择动作后施加，且受累积预算约束；通过理论分析建立遗憾上界，包括期望和高概率保证；作为特例分析无扰动情况（经典线性赌博机优化）

Result: 建立了该模型下的期望和高概率遗憾上界；作为特例，为经典线性赌博机优化改进了高概率遗憾界；通过下界证明补充了上界结果，表明所获上界在某种意义下是紧的

Conclusion: 该研究为具有非凸非光滑损失函数和扰动项的对抗性赌博机优化问题提供了理论框架和保证，扩展了经典线性赌博机优化的结果，并通过上下界分析建立了问题的理论复杂度

Abstract: We study a class of adversarial bandit optimization problems in which the loss functions may be non-convex and non-smooth. In each round, the learner observes a loss that consists of an underlying linear component together with an additional perturbation applied after the learner selects an action. The perturbations are measured relative to the linear losses and are constrained by a global budget that bounds their cumulative magnitude over time. Under this model, we establish both expected and high-probability regret guarantees. As a special case of our analysis, we recover an improved high-probability regret bound for classical bandit linear optimization, which corresponds to the setting without perturbations. We further complement our upper bounds by proving a lower bound on the expected regret.

</details>


### [143] [Selective Deficits in LLM Mental Self-Modeling in a Behavior-Based Test of Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26089)
*Christopher Ackerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种新的实验范式来测试LLMs是否真正掌握了心理理论能力，发现2025年中期之前的LLMs完全失败，近期LLMs在他人心理状态建模上达到人类水平，但前沿LLMs在自我建模任务上仍然失败，除非提供推理痕迹作为"草稿纸"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究LLMs是否真正学会了心理理论（Theory of Mind）的因果模型，还是仅仅模仿训练数据中的模式。心理理论是人类理解和预测他人心理状态的能力，对社交互动至关重要。虽然LLMs在训练数据中接触过无数心理理论的例子，但它们是否能在任意环境中部署因果模型尚不清楚。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）开发新颖的实验范式，要求被试形成自我和他人的心理状态表征并进行战略性行动，而不仅仅是描述；2）测试2024年以来发布的各种领先的开源和闭源LLMs；3）测试人类被试作为基准；4）探索认知负荷对他人建模任务的影响；5）分析推理模型在自我和他人建模任务中的机制；6）考察LLMs是否进行战略性欺骗。

Result: 研究结果发现：1）2025年中期之前发布的LLMs在所有任务上都失败；2）近期LLMs在他人认知状态建模上达到人类水平；3）即使是前沿LLMs在自我建模任务上也失败，除非提供推理痕迹作为"草稿纸"；4）认知负荷对他人建模任务有影响，表明LLMs可能使用类似有限容量工作记忆的机制来保持心理表征；5）推理模型在自我和他人建模任务中容易进行战略性欺骗。

Conclusion: 结论是LLMs在心理理论能力方面取得了显著进步，特别是在他人心理状态建模方面已达到人类水平，但在自我建模方面仍有局限。研究还表明LLMs可能使用类似工作记忆的机制来处理心理表征，并且能够进行战略性欺骗。这些发现对理解LLMs的社会认知能力和潜在应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: The ability to represent oneself and others as agents with knowledge, intentions, and belief states that guide their behavior - Theory of Mind - is a human universal that enables us to navigate - and manipulate - the social world. It is supported by our ability to form mental models of ourselves and others. Its ubiquity in human affairs entails that LLMs have seen innumerable examples of it in their training data and therefore may have learned to mimic it, but whether they have actually learned causal models that they can deploy in arbitrary settings is unclear. We therefore develop a novel experimental paradigm that requires that subjects form representations of the mental states of themselves and others and act on them strategically rather than merely describe them. We test a wide range of leading open and closed source LLMs released since 2024, as well as human subjects, on this paradigm. We find that 1) LLMs released before mid-2025 fail at all of our tasks, 2) more recent LLMs achieve human-level performance on modeling the cognitive states of others, and 3) even frontier LLMs fail at our self-modeling task - unless afforded a scratchpad in the form of a reasoning trace. We further demonstrate cognitive load effects on other-modeling tasks, offering suggestive evidence that LLMs are using something akin to limited-capacity working memory to hold these mental representations in mind during a single forward pass. Finally, we explore the mechanisms by which reasoning models succeed at the self- and other-modeling tasks, and show that they readily engage in strategic deception.

</details>


### [144] [Accurate Precipitation Forecast by Efficiently Learning from Massive Atmospheric Variables and Unbalanced Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26108)
*Shuangliang Li,Siwei Li,Li Li,Weijie Zou,Jie Yang,Maolin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新型短期降水预报模型，能有效利用海量大气观测数据，通过自动提取与迭代预测降水演化的潜在特征，结合WMCE损失函数提高对稀少降水事件的识别精度，在准确性和计算效率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期降水预报对社会经济和公共安全至关重要，但面临三大挑战：降水演化模式高度复杂、降水与非降水样本极度不平衡、现有模型无法高效利用海量多源大气观测数据，这限制了预报精度和计算效率的提升。

Method: 开发了一种新型预报模型，能够有效且高效地利用海量大气观测数据，通过自动提取与迭代预测与降水演化强相关的潜在特征。同时引入了"WMCE"损失函数，旨在准确区分极端稀少的降水事件并精确预测其强度值。

Result: 在两个数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的模型在准确性和效率方面都显著且持续地优于所有流行基线方法。此外，与现有方法相比，该预报模型大幅降低了获得有价值预测所需的计算成本。

Conclusion: 该模型为高效实用的降水预报树立了里程碑，通过创新的特征提取机制和损失函数设计，解决了短期降水预报中的关键挑战，在保持高精度的同时大幅提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Short-term (0-24 hours) precipitation forecasting is highly valuable to socioeconomic activities and public safety. However, the highly complex evolution patterns of precipitation events, the extreme imbalance between precipitation and non-precipitation samples, and the inability of existing models to efficiently and effectively utilize large volumes of multi-source atmospheric observation data hinder improvements in precipitation forecasting accuracy and computational efficiency. To address the above challenges, this study developed a novel forecasting model capable of effectively and efficiently utilizing massive atmospheric observations by automatically extracting and iteratively predicting the latent features strongly associated with precipitation evolution. Furthermore, this study introduces a 'WMCE' loss function, designed to accurately discriminate extremely scarce precipitation events while precisely predicting their intensity values. Extensive experiments on two datasets demonstrate that our proposed model substantially and consistently outperforms all prevalent baselines in both accuracy and efficiency. Moreover, the proposed forecasting model substantially lowers the computational cost required to obtain valuable predictions compared to existing approaches, thereby positioning it as a milestone for efficient and practical precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [145] [On the Complexity of Optimal Graph Rewiring for Oversmoothing and Oversquashing in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26140)
*Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从计算复杂性角度研究了GNN深度扩展中的图结构优化问题，证明了缓解过平滑和过挤压的图优化问题是NP难的


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在深度架构中面临过平滑（节点表示趋同）和过挤压（远距离信息传播失败）两大挑战，这些现象与底层图结构密切相关，因此需要研究能否通过优化图拓扑来缓解这些问题

Method: 将过平滑和过挤压缓解分别形式化为基于谱间隙和电导率的图优化问题，通过从最小二分问题归约，证明这两个问题的精确优化都是NP难的

Result: 证明了过平滑和过挤压缓解的图优化问题的精确优化是NP难的，其决策版本是NP完全的，为理解图重连优化的基本限制提供了理论基础

Conclusion: 研究结果为理解图重连优化GNN的基本限制提供了理论依据，并证明了在实际中需要使用近似算法和启发式方法的合理性

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) face two fundamental challenges when scaled to deep architectures: oversmoothing, where node representations converge to indistinguishable vectors, and oversquashing, where information from distant nodes fails to propagate through bottlenecks. Both phenomena are intimately tied to the underlying graph structure, raising a natural question: can we optimize the graph topology to mitigate these issues? This paper provides a theoretical investigation of the computational complexity of such graph structure optimization. We formulate oversmoothing and oversquashing mitigation as graph optimization problems based on spectral gap and conductance, respectively. We prove that exact optimization for either problem is NP-hard through reductions from Minimum Bisection, establishing NP-completeness of the decision versions. Our results provide theoretical foundations for understanding the fundamental limits of graph rewiring for GNN optimization and justify the use of approximation algorithms and heuristic methods in practice.

</details>


### [146] [DataFlex: A Unified Framework for Data-Centric Dynamic Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26164)
*Hao Liang,Zhengyang Zhao,Meiyi Qiang,Mingrui Chen,Lu Ma,Rongyi Yu,Hengyi Feng,Shixuan Sun,Zimo Meng,Xiaochen Ma,Xuanlin Yang,Qifeng Cai,Ruichuan An,Bohan Zeng,Zhen Hao Wong,Chengyu Shen,Runming He,Zhaoyang Han,Yaowei Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Conghui He,Bin Cui,Zhiyu Li,Weinan E,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DataFlex是一个基于LLaMA-Factory的统一数据中心动态训练框架，支持样本选择、领域混合调整和样本重加权三种数据优化范式，旨在解决现有方法在代码库隔离、接口不一致方面的问题，提升LLM训练的复现性、公平比较和实际集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择、数据混合优化和数据重加权方法通常在隔离的代码库中开发，接口不一致，这阻碍了复现性、公平比较和实际集成。需要统一的框架来支持数据中心的动态训练。

Method: 基于LLaMA-Factory构建DataFlex框架，支持三种动态数据优化范式：样本选择、领域混合调整和样本重加权。提供可扩展的训练器抽象和模块化组件，统一嵌入提取、推理和梯度计算等关键模型相关操作，支持DeepSpeed ZeRO-3等大规模设置。

Result: 动态数据选择在MMLU上始终优于静态全数据训练（Mistral-7B和Llama-3.2-3B）。对于数据混合，DoReMi和ODM在6B和30B token规模上预训练Qwen2.5-1.5B时，相比默认比例提高了MMLU准确率和语料级困惑度。DataFlex相比原始实现实现了持续的运行时间改进。

Conclusion: DataFlex为LLM的数据中心动态训练提供了一个有效、高效且可复现的基础设施，解决了现有方法的碎片化问题，支持多种数据优化范式并与标准训练工作流完全兼容。

Abstract: Data-centric training has emerged as a promising direction for improving large language models (LLMs) by optimizing not only model parameters but also the selection, composition, and weighting of training data during optimization. However, existing approaches to data selection, data mixture optimization, and data reweighting are often developed in isolated codebases with inconsistent interfaces, hindering reproducibility, fair comparison, and practical integration. In this paper, we present DataFlex, a unified data-centric dynamic training framework built upon LLaMA-Factory. DataFlex supports three major paradigms of dynamic data optimization: sample selection, domain mixture adjustment, and sample reweighting, while remaining fully compatible with the original training workflow. It provides extensible trainer abstractions and modular components, enabling a drop-in replacement for standard LLM training, and unifies key model-dependent operations such as embedding extraction, inference, and gradient computation, with support for large-scale settings including DeepSpeed ZeRO-3. We conduct comprehensive experiments across multiple data-centric methods. Dynamic data selection consistently outperforms static full-data training on MMLU across both Mistral-7B and Llama-3.2-3B. For data mixture, DoReMi and ODM improve both MMLU accuracy and corpus-level perplexity over default proportions when pretraining Qwen2.5-1.5B on SlimPajama at 6B and 30B token scales. DataFlex also achieves consistent runtime improvements over original implementations. These results demonstrate that DataFlex provides an effective, efficient, and reproducible infrastructure for data-centric dynamic training of LLMs.

</details>


### [147] [Knowledge Distillation for Efficient Transformer-Based Reinforcement Learning in Hardware-Constrained Energy Management Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26249)
*Pascal Henrich,Jonas Sievers,Maximilian Beichter,Thomas Blank,Ralf Mikut,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 知识蒸馏将大型决策变压器策略压缩为小型模型，用于住宅能源管理，在保持控制性能的同时大幅减少模型大小和推理时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变压器模型在住宅能源管理中表现出色，但计算需求过高，不适合资源受限的嵌入式控制器部署。需要将大型模型的决策能力转移到紧凑模型中。

Method: 使用Ausgrid数据集在离线序列决策变压器框架中训练教师模型，然后通过匹配教师动作来蒸馏较小的学生模型，实现知识转移。

Result: 蒸馏在保持控制性能（甚至提升1%）的同时，将参数减少96%，推理内存减少90%，推理时间减少63%。相同架构容量的学生模型也能获得可比成本改进。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏使决策变压器控制更适合资源受限硬件的住宅能源管理应用，实现了性能保持与效率提升的平衡。

Abstract: Transformer-based reinforcement learning has emerged as a strong candidate for sequential control in residential energy management. In particular, the Decision Transformer can learn effective battery dispatch policies from historical data, thereby increasing photovoltaic self-consumption and reducing electricity costs. However, transformer models are typically too computationally demanding for deployment on resource-constrained residential controllers, where memory and latency constraints are critical. This paper investigates knowledge distillation to transfer the decision-making behaviour of high-capacity Decision Transformer policies to compact models that are more suitable for embedded deployment. Using the Ausgrid dataset, we train teacher models in an offline sequence-based Decision Transformer framework on heterogeneous multi-building data. We then distil smaller student models by matching the teachers' actions, thereby preserving control quality while reducing model size. Across a broad set of teacher-student configurations, distillation largely preserves control performance and even yields small improvements of up to 1%, while reducing the parameter count by up to 96%, the inference memory by up to 90%, and the inference time by up to 63%. Beyond these compression effects, comparable cost improvements are also observed when distilling into a student model of identical architectural capacity. Overall, our results show that knowledge distillation makes Decision Transformer control more applicable for residential energy management on resource-limited hardware.

</details>


### [148] [Improving Risk Stratification in Hypertrophic Cardiomyopathy: A Novel Score Combining Echocardiography, Clinical, and Medication Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26254)
*Marion Taconné,Valentina D. A. Corino,Annamaria Del Franco,Sara Giovani,Iacopo Olivotto,Adrien Al Wazzan,Erwan Donal,Pietro Cerveri,Luca Mainardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发基于机器学习的可解释风险评分模型，利用常规临床数据预测肥厚型心肌病患者5年心血管复合终点，显著优于现有ESC评分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肥厚型心肌病需要准确的风险分层来指导ICD治疗和随访管理，现有ESC评分模型区分性能中等，需要更精准的预测工具

Method: 利用电子健康记录中的常规超声心动图、临床和药物数据，开发随机森林集成模型，在SHARE注册中心队列（N=1,201）训练和内部验证，在Rennes医院独立队列（N=382）进行外部验证

Result: 模型内部AUC达0.85±0.02，显著优于ESC评分（0.56±0.03）；外部验证显示ML评分风险分层更优（Log-rank p=8.62×10⁻⁴ vs ESC p=0.0559）；纵向分析显示模型在无事件患者中保持稳定

Conclusion: 该可解释机器学习风险评分模型在预测肥厚型心肌病心血管结局方面优于现有方法，其高可解释性和纵向风险监测能力为个性化临床管理提供了有前景的工具

Abstract: Hypertrophic cardiomyopathy (HCM) requires accurate risk stratification to inform decisions regarding ICD therapy and follow-up management. Current established models, such as the European Society of Cardiology (ESC) score, exhibit moderate discriminative performance. This study develops a robust, explainable machine learning (ML) risk score leveraging routinely collected echocardiographic, clinical, and medication data, typically contained within Electronic Health Records (EHRs), to predict a 5-year composite cardiovascular outcome in HCM patients. The model was trained and internally validated using a large cohort (N=1,201) from the SHARE registry (Florence Hospital) and externally validated on an independent cohort (N=382) from Rennes Hospital. The final Random Forest ensemble model achieved a high internal Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 0.85 +- 0.02, significantly outperforming the ESC score (0.56 +- 0.03). Critically, survival curve analysis on the external validation set showed superior risk separation for the ML score (Log-rank p = 8.62 x 10^(-4) compared to the ESC score (p = 0.0559).
